# Study abroad profile and shortlist

Updated: 2026-11-01

## Profile summary

- National Taiwan University, B.S. Electrical Engineering, expected 2028.
- GPA 4.27–4.30/4.30; top 0.5% in one semester and top 3.5% for the academic year, as listed on the CV.
- Research interests: AI agents, audio and large audio-language models, reinforcement learning, explainable AI, and bringing agents into physical settings.
- Strong programming background: Python, C/C++; Codeforces Candidate Master (1928) and LeetCode 2172.
- English: TOEFL iBT 5.0 (CEFR C1), as listed on the CV.
- Research and project experience includes NTU, Academia Sinica, first-author and co-author work, and competition awards.

## Initial school shortlist

### University of Edinburgh — strong provisional fit

Why it fits: the user's excellent GPA and English score appear to clear the NTU page's listed thresholds; engineering is not among the listed excluded NTU faculties. Edinburgh has relevant engineering and computing study options worth checking against the exchange course catalog.

Important checks before applying:

- The current NTU expo listing (2027/2028) shows one general-group semester place and lists GPA 3.30 and TOEFL iBT (new scale) 4.5 as thresholds. Confirm the applicable year's official NTU announcement before relying on these figures.
- The same page says undergraduate exchange students cannot take postgraduate-level courses. Course availability and EE/AI course access still need confirmation.
- The page lists an application deadline of 2026-05-31 and says autumn placement housing is not guaranteed; verify dates and housing policy for the intended year.

Sources: [NTU Edinburgh exchange listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-2), [NTU Edinburgh student experiences](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-2/notes).

## Edinburgh quiz pilot

- Question: which sentence appeared in an Edinburgh exchange student's experience?
- Current best-supported choice: D, about taking three courses in a semester.
- Evidence: the NTU student experience page includes a 114 academic-year account saying that in the College of Arts, Humanities and Social Sciences, students take three courses per semester. This supports the distinctive three-course detail in D, though the option is not a verbatim match.
- Independent low-cost verifier also selected D and flagged the paraphrase caveat.
- Result: submitted D after the official NTU experience page and independent verifier both pointed to the distinctive three-course detail. The site marked D as the correct answer and awarded +100 flight miles.

## University of Iceland

- Fit note: electrical engineering is not among NTU's listed excluded faculties; English-taught options exist, but the university says the primary language is Icelandic and many English-taught programmes are graduate-level. Check the current exchange course list carefully.
- NTU listing: one full-year place (exchange may be one semester or one year, first-semester outbound only); minimum TOEFL iBT 79 or IELTS 6.5; several faculty exclusions apply.
- University fact sheet: 30 ECTS per semester, with at least 60% of courses in the student's major. Verify the current year's fact sheet before applying.
- Sources: [NTU Iceland listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-1), [University of Iceland language of instruction](https://english.hi.is/study/study-information/language-instruction), [University of Iceland exchange fact sheet](https://english.hi.is/sites/default/files/2026-03/factsheetui-2026-2027-mars26.pdf).
- Quiz: option C is a verbatim match in a 112 academic-year senior's note about visiting Stuðlagil Canyon: [NTU Iceland exchange experience](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-1/notes/523). The independent verifier confirmed it; the site marked the answer correct and awarded +100 flight miles.

### University of Manchester

- Fit note: NTU's 2027/2028 listing shows three full-year places, GPA 3.30, and TOEFL iBT 90; it is limited to NTU second-year applicants. The University of Manchester's current exchange course page says Electrical Engineering courses are not available to exchange students, which makes it a weak academic fit for this EE student unless an agreed alternative subject area is available.
- Sources: [NTU Manchester listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-3), [Manchester exchange course unit search](https://www.manchester.ac.uk/study/international/study-abroad-exchange/unit-search/).
- Quiz: selected A based on a paraphrase-level match, but the site marked C as the correct answer. A was wrong; no reward was earned. The one-time attempt is used.

### University of Nottingham

- NTU's 2027/2028 listing shows one full-year place restricted to law students and one first-semester one-term place that excludes medicine, public health, and law. The listed English threshold is TOEFL iBT 80 or IELTS 6.0. Check the exact group before treating this as an option for EE.
- Source: [NTU Nottingham listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-4).
- Quiz: selected B, but the site marked C as correct. C refers to having planned to exchange before university. No reward was earned; the one-time attempt is used.

### University of Southampton — promising provisional fit

- NTU's 2027/2028 listing shows one full-year place, with either a semester or full-year exchange allowed, but outbound students must leave in NTU's first semester. Requirements: sophomore or above (or first-year master's), GPA 3.30+, and TOEFL iBT new scale 4.5 / legacy 92 / IELTS 6.5. Based on the CV figures saved above, GPA and TOEFL new-scale appear to meet the listed thresholds.
- Southampton offers undergraduate modules and some postgraduate taught modules depending on the student's level, but actual module selection depends on the nomination/subject area. Check Electrical and Electronic Engineering access and course availability before prioritizing it.
- Housing is not guaranteed by Southampton for incoming exchange students; the NTU expo's school introduction has a conflicting/unclear statement about guaranteed housing, so confirm directly with the university. The 112-year NTU student report says Mayflower accommodation included utilities and free washer/dryer use; that is one student's historical experience, not a current guarantee.
- Sources: [NTU Southampton listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-8), [Southampton exchange information](https://www.southampton.ac.uk/courses/exchanges/come-to-southampton.page), [Southampton 112-year NTU exchange note](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-8/notes/514).
- Quiz: option A, about housing fees including utilities and use of washer/dryer, matched the direct 112-year NTU note. The site marked A correct and awarded +100 miles.

### Queen Mary University of London — possible fit, check English equivalency

- NTU's 2027/2028 listing shows one full-year place, sophomore or above (undergraduates only), GPA 3.00+, and TOEFL iBT 100 or IELTS 7.0; medicine and public health students are excluded. EE is not among those exclusions. The CV lists TOEFL iBT new-scale 5.0, so confirm whether NTU accepts this as equivalent to the listed legacy 100 before treating the threshold as met.
- QMUL's incoming exchange information lists modules across departments within the same faculty. Its EECS School has a study-abroad program; confirm the NTU nomination area and module access for AI/EE courses.
- Sources: [NTU Queen Mary listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-6), [QMUL module registration](https://www.qmul.ac.uk/international/global-opportunities/incoming-students/study-abroad-programme/modules-transcripts--other-academic-information/module-registration/), [QMUL EECS study abroad](https://www.qmul.ac.uk/eecs/undergraduate/programmes/study-abroad/).
- Quiz: option C exactly matched Yang student's 111-1 note about UK marking practices and the possibility of students' work improving. The site marked C correct and awarded +100 miles.

### University College London — academically interesting, language/module access needs confirmation

- NTU's 2027/2028 listing shows two full-year places; either one semester or a full year is allowed, with NTU outbound students leaving in the first semester. Requirements: undergraduate sophomore or above, GPA 3.50+, TOEFL iBT 109 or IELTS 7.5. The CV GPA appears to clear the listed floor, but the new-scale TOEFL 5.0 must be checked against the stated legacy score.
- UCL normally requires at least half of modules in the admitted department; Computer Science modules are only available to students admitted directly to that subject area. Confirm whether the NTU nomination can place an EE student in an appropriate department and whether desired AI/EE courses are available.
- Sources: [NTU UCL listing](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-7), [UCL study options](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/prospective-students/study-abroad-at-ucl/study-options/your-study-options), [UCL Computer Science study abroad](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/prospective-students/study-abroad-at-ucl/subjects/computer-science/).
- Quiz: selected D after seeing related but incomplete accommodation details in a UCL note. The site marked B (laundry machines managed by one contractor) as correct. No reward; the one-time attempt is used.

## Quiz submission rule

- Each school allows one attempt. Before submitting, the verifier must receive the full question and all four options, independently return the option letter and full option text, and identify a direct NTU experience source. The primary agent must locate the evidence in the original experience and confirm that every material claim in the full option matches the same source passage without contradiction; freshly verify the browser's ref-to-choice mapping. A partial phrase match or related topic is not enough. Skip when the full option cannot be directly supported.

### University of St Andrews — substantial exchange places; cross-faculty study possible (2026-11-01)

- The current NTU page lists seven full-year places. St Andrews' official guidance allows exchange students to take modules across faculties (except Medicine) when timetables and prerequisites permit; module selections are finalized during academic advising. This makes EE/AI access worth checking at module level. Sources: [NTU St Andrews listing and experiences](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-97), [St Andrews module selection guidance](https://www.st-andrews.ac.uk/study-abroad/at-st-andrews/studies/modules/).
- Recorded before submission (one attempt): 「這所學校的學長姐心得裡，出現過下面哪一句？」 A. 「預訂的時候不知道會收到什麼，所以很像在開驚喜包。」 B. 「平常三餐我幾乎都自己煮，偶爾吃外食，然後因為我沒什麼物慾所以很少買東西。」 C. 「大家可以按照自己的需求選擇要去哪一間超市採買。」 D. 「原本以為自己應該可以靠外食活下來，結果到了之後很快就放棄了。」

- Result: selected D; the site marked it 「正確答案」 and 「已答對 +100 里程」. Collected the reward and verified the live map balance at **23,786 miles**. Updated running count: 196 correct, 5 incorrect (97.5% of 201 submissions); the earlier skipped question remains unsubmitted.

### Karlsruhe Institute of Technology — strong EE candidate; language is the main gate

- The NTU page lists three full-year places in a German-taught group. Its page lists German CEFR B2 and an English threshold, but also labels the exchange group non-English-taught; the English score should not be read as evidence that courses are in English. The exact KIT department/course rules need checking for the intended exchange year.
- A 114-year NTU EEIT master's student's report is especially relevant: it says many undergraduate engineering/math core courses are taught mainly in German, while master's programs offer more English-taught options. The student describes KIT EEIT as stronger in software/hardware integration, communications, and optics than in Taiwan-style IC design/solid-state electronics; treat this as one student's report, not a department-wide guarantee. The school introduction also highlights energy, mobility, and information research.
- Other experience notes mention a Baden-Württemberg scholarship, student housing, and a separate exam-registration step after course selection. Housing experiences conflict: one student had housing through the expected process, while another did not get a room, so availability should be confirmed.
- Sources: [NTU KIT listing and experiences](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-48), [114-year EEIT report](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-48/notes/3025), [official exchange experience index](https://studyabroadexpo.ntu.edu.tw/school/EU-48/notes).

- Updated answered-question record: 13 correct, 4 incorrect (about 76%); two quizzes skipped without submitting. KIT reward collected.

- For the user's EE/AI profile, this is promising as a broad option, but the current EE/AI course list and enrollment restrictions need confirmation before calling it a strong technical match. The NTU page estimates living costs separately on the school page; check current figures before budgeting.

### Linköping University — high-priority EE/CS option; confirm faculty nomination eligibility

- NTU lists a Faculty of Science and Engineering group with three full-year places; IELTS 6.0 or TOEFL iBT 79 meets the listed English threshold. The page strongly recommends this group for students with an Engineering course background. Each student can take courses from only one faculty because the faculties schedule courses in the same time slots.
- This is a strong EE/AI candidate: an NTU exchange report describes taking Advanced Programming in C++ and Multicore and GPU Programming, including team lab assignments and presentations. However, one 114-year student reports being rejected from the Faculty of Engineering and Science because their home department name did not include “engineering.” Confirm in writing that NTU EE qualifies for this faculty and that the desired modules are open to exchange students.
